<script type="text/javascript">
    var nostoName = 'nostojs';

    // Disable Nosto fty before slick and jquery is initialized
    require([nostoName], function () {
        (function () {
            window[nostoName] = window[nostoName] || function (cb) {
                (window[nostoName].q = window[nostoName].q || []).push(cb);
            };
        })();

        window[nostoName](function (api) {
            api.setAutoLoad(false);
        });
    });

    // Listen for populated recommendations and trigger resize
    require(['jquery', 'slick', 'alignItems', nostoName], function ($) {
        nostojs(function(api){
            api.listen('postrender', function(nostoPostRenderEvent) {
                var $body = $('body'),
                    $window = $(window);

                if (nostoPostRenderEvent.filledElements.length > 0 && 'MutationObserver' in window) {
                    var $wrapper = $('#' + nostoPostRenderEvent.filledElements[0]);

                    if ($wrapper.length > 0) {
                        var observer = new MutationObserver(function(mutations) {
                            mutations.forEach(function(mutation) {
                                if (mutation.type === 'attributes' && mutation.target.className.indexOf('slick-initialized') !== -1) {
                                    if (!$wrapper.hasClass('initialized')) {
                                        $wrapper.trigger('nostoElementInitialized');
                                    }

                                    $wrapper.addClass('initialized');
                                    $window.trigger('resize');
                                }
                            });
                        });

                        observer.observe($wrapper.find('ol[class]')[0], {attributes: true});
                    }
                }

                $body.trigger('contentUpdated');
            });
        });
    });
</script>
<script type="text/javascript">
    require([
        'jquery'
    ], function ($) {
        var lazySourceAttrSelector = 'data-lazy-load';

        // Image lazy load jquery plugin
        $.fn.unveil = function () {
            var $w = $(window),
                images = this,
                loaded;

            this.one('unveil', function () {
                var source = this.getAttribute(lazySourceAttrSelector);

                if (source) {
                    var img = $(this);

                    this.setAttribute('src', source);

                    img.load(function () {
                        img.addClass('lazy-loaded').removeAttr(lazySourceAttrSelector);
                    });
                }
            });

            function unveil() {
                var inview = images.filter(function () {
                    var $e = $(this);

                    if ($e.is(':hidden')) return;

                    var wt = $w.scrollTop(),
                        wb = wt + $w.height(),
                        et = $e.offset().top,
                        eb = et + $e.height();

                    return eb >= wt && et <= wb;
                });

                loaded = inview.trigger('unveil');
                images = images.not(loaded);

                if (images.length <= 0) {
                    $(window).off('unveil');
                }
            }

            $w.on('scroll.unveil resize.unveil lookup.unveil', unveil);
            unveil();

            return this;
        };

        // Block lazy load jquery plugin
        $.fn.lazyLoadBlock = function (callback) {
            var $w = $(window),
                blocks = this,
                th = 100,
                loaded;

            this.one('lazyLoadBlock', function () {
                if (typeof callback === 'function') callback.call(this);
            });

            function lazyLoadBlock() {
                var inview = blocks.filter(function () {
                    var $e = $(this);

                    if ($e.is(':hidden')) return;

                    var wt = $w.scrollTop(),
                        wb = wt + $w.height(),
                        et = $e.offset().top,
                        eb = et + $e.height();

                    return eb >= wt - th && et <= wb + th;
                });

                loaded = inview.trigger('lazyLoadBlock');
                blocks = blocks.not(loaded);
            }

            $w.on('scroll.lazyLoadBlock resize.lazyLoadBlock lookup.lazyLoadBlock', lazyLoadBlock);
            lazyLoadBlock();

            return this;
        };
    });
</script>
<script>
    try {
        if (!window.localStorage || !window.sessionStorage) {
            throw new Error();
        }

        localStorage.setItem('storage_test', 1);
        localStorage.removeItem('storage_test');
    } catch(e) {
        (function () {
            var Storage = function (type) {
                var data;

                function createCookie(name, value, days) {
                    var date, expires;

                    if (days) {
                        date = new Date();
                        date.setTime(date.getTime()+(days * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000));
                        expires = '; expires=' + date.toGMTString();
                    } else {
                        expires = '';
                    }
                    document.cookie = name + '=' + value+expires+'; path=/';
                }

                function readCookie(name) {
                    var nameEQ = name + '=',
                        ca = document.cookie.split(';'),
                        i = 0,
                        c;

                    for (i=0; i < ca.length; i++) {
                        c = ca[i];

                        while (c.charAt(0) === ' ') {
                            c = c.substring(1,c.length);
                        }

                        if (c.indexOf(nameEQ) === 0) {
                            return c.substring(nameEQ.length, c.length);
                        }
                    }

                    return null;
                }

                function setData(data) {
                    data = encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify(data));
                    createCookie(type === 'session' ? getSessionName() : 'localStorage', data, 365);
                }

                function clearData() {
                    createCookie(type === 'session' ? getSessionName() : 'localStorage', '', 365);
                }

                function getData() {
                    var data = type === 'session' ? readCookie(getSessionName()) : readCookie('localStorage');

                    return data ? JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(data)) : {};
                }

                function getSessionName() {
                    if (!window.name) {
                        window.name = new Date().getTime();
                    }

                    return 'sessionStorage' + window.name;
                }

                data = getData();

                return {
                    length: 0,
                    clear: function () {
                        data = {};
                        this.length = 0;
                        clearData();
                    },

                    getItem: function (key) {
                        return data[key] === undefined ? null : data[key];
                    },

                    key: function (i) {
                        var ctr = 0,
                            k;

                        for (k in data) {
                            if (ctr.toString() === i.toString()) {
                                return k;
                            } else {
                                ctr++
                            }
                        }

                        return null;
                    },

                    removeItem: function (key) {
                        delete data[key];
                        this.length--;
                        setData(data);
                    },

                    setItem: function (key, value) {
                        data[key] = value.toString();
                        this.length++;
                        setData(data);
                    }
                };
            };

            window.localStorage.__proto__ = window.localStorage = new Storage('local');
            window.sessionStorage.__proto__ = window.sessionStorage = new Storage('session');
        })();
    }
</script>
<script>
        require.config({
            deps: [
                'jquery',
                'mage/translate',
                'jquery/jquery-storageapi'
            ],
            callback: function ($) {
                'use strict';

                var dependencies = [],
                    versionObj;

                $.initNamespaceStorage('mage-translation-storage');
                $.initNamespaceStorage('mage-translation-file-version');
                versionObj = $.localStorage.get('mage-translation-file-version');

                
                if (versionObj.version !== '97485b4278bc9b1e9c78ef890d10e8a888ff11f0') {
                    dependencies.push(
                        'text!js-translation.json'
                    );

                }

                require.config({
                    deps: dependencies,
                    callback: function (string) {
                        if (typeof string === 'string') {
                            $.mage.translate.add(JSON.parse(string));
                            $.localStorage.set('mage-translation-storage', string);
                            $.localStorage.set(
                                'mage-translation-file-version',
                                {
                                    version: '97485b4278bc9b1e9c78ef890d10e8a888ff11f0'
                                }
                            );
                        } else {
                            $.mage.translate.add($.localStorage.get('mage-translation-storage'));
                        }
                    }
                });
            }
        });
    </script>

<script type="text/javascript">
        // Handle cookie global variables
        var cookies = JSON.parse(getCookies('user_allowed_save_cookie'));

        function getCookies(name) {
            var arg = name + '=',
                aLength = arg.length,
                cookie = document.cookie,
                cLength = cookie.length,
                i = 0,
                j = 0;

            while (i < cLength) {
                j = i + aLength;

                if (cookie.substring(i, j) === arg) {
                    return getCookieVal(j);
                }
                i = cookie.indexOf(' ', i) + 1;

                if (i === 0) {
                    break;
                }
            }

            return null;
        }

        function getCookieVal(offset) {
            var cookie = document.cookie,
                endstr = cookie.indexOf(';', offset);

            if (endstr === -1) {
                endstr = cookie.length;
            }

            return decodeURIComponent(cookie.substring(offset, endstr));
        }

        window.bmx = window.bmx || {};
        bmx.cookieConsent = {};

        if (!cookies || cookies.cookies.length === 0) {
            bmx.cookieConsent = {
                marketing: true,
                analytics: true
            };
        } else {
            bmx.cookieConsent.analytics = cookies.cookies.indexOf('analytic_cookie') > -1;
            bmx.cookieConsent.marketing = cookies.cookies.indexOf('marketing_cookie') > -1;
        }
    </script>

<script>
        require(['jquery', 'mage/cookies'], function ($) {
            'use strict';

            updateBlock();

            $('#main_customer_type_switcher button').on('click', function (e) {
                e.preventDefault();

                // Exception for credit form to remove parameter
                var location = window.location,
                    path = location.pathname + location.search + location.hash,
                    route = location.pathname.split('/')[1],
                    url = location.href.replace(path, '') + '/';

                $('#main_customer_type_switcher input').val($(this).data('type'));

                // Excception for collector checkout to use ajax
                if (route === 'collectorcheckout') {
                    $.ajax({
                        url: 'https://www.byggmax.no/customertype/type/switch/',
                        data: $('#main_customer_type_switcher form').serialize(),
                        type: 'POST',
                        dataType: 'json',
                        beforeSend: function () {
                            $('body').trigger('collector:pause');
                        },
                        complete: function () {
                            updateBlock();
                            $('#main_customer_type_switcher').trigger('contentUpdated');
                            $('body').trigger('collector:resume');
                        }
                    });
                } else {
                    $('#main_customer_type_switcher form').submit();
                }
            });

            $('body').on('typeSwitcher:updateBlock', function () {
                updateBlock();
            });

            function updateBlock() {
                if ($.mage.cookies.get('customer-type') === '1') {
                    $('.type-private').removeClass('active');
                    $('.type-company').addClass('active');
                } else {
                    $('.type-private').addClass('active');
                    $('.type-company').removeClass('active');
                }
            }
        });
    </script>

                require([
                    'jquery',
                    'matchMedia'
                ], function ($) {
                    var hoursWrapper =  $('.hours-wrapper.store-not-selected'),
                        checkHoursWrapper = function () {
                            if ($(hoursWrapper).hasClass('active')) {
                                $('body').click();
                            } else {
                                $(hoursWrapper).addClass('active')
                            }
                        };

                    mediaCheck({
                        media: '(max-width: 1023px)',
                        entry: function () {
                            checkHoursWrapper();
                        },
                        exit: function () {
                            checkHoursWrapper();
                        }
                    });
                });

<div class="customer-type-switcher" id="secondary_customer_type_switcher">
<div>
<button class="type-private active" data-type="0">Privat</button>
</div>
<span>/</span>
<div>
<button class="type-company" data-type="1">Bedrift</button>
</div>
<form action="https://www.byggmax.no/customertype/type/switch/" class="hidden" method="post">
<input name="type" type="text" value="0"/>
</form>
</div>
<script>
        require(['jquery', 'mage/cookies'], function ($) {
            'use strict';

            updateBlock();

            $('#secondary_customer_type_switcher button').on('click', function (e) {
                e.preventDefault();

                // Exception for credit form to remove parameter
                var location = window.location,
                    path = location.pathname + location.search + location.hash,
                    route = location.pathname.split('/')[1],
                    url = location.href.replace(path, '') + '/';

                $('#secondary_customer_type_switcher input').val($(this).data('type'));

                // Excception for collector checkout to use ajax
                if (route === 'collectorcheckout') {
                    $.ajax({
                        url: 'https://www.byggmax.no/customertype/type/switch/',
                        data: $('#secondary_customer_type_switcher form').serialize(),
                        type: 'POST',
                        dataType: 'json',
                        beforeSend: function () {
                            $('body').trigger('collector:pause');
                        },
                        complete: function () {
                            updateBlock();
                            $('#secondary_customer_type_switcher').trigger('contentUpdated');
                            $('body').trigger('collector:resume');
                        }
                    });
                } else {
                    $('#secondary_customer_type_switcher form').submit();
                }
            });

            $('body').on('typeSwitcher:updateBlock', function () {
                updateBlock();
            });

            function updateBlock() {
                if ($.mage.cookies.get('customer-type') === '1') {
                    $('.type-private').removeClass('active');
                    $('.type-company').addClass('active');
                } else {
                    $('.type-private').addClass('active');
                    $('.type-company').removeClass('active');
                }
            }
        });
    </script>

<script type="text/javascript">
            require([
                'jquery',
                'mage/dropdown',
                'matchMedia'
            ], function ($) {
                var minicart = $('.minicart-wrapper'),
                    minicartBlock = $(minicart).find('[data-role="dropdownDialog"]'),
                    scrollHtml = 0,
                    html = $('html'),
                    body = $('body');

                mediaCheck({
                    media: '(max-width: 1023px)',
                    entry: function () {
                        if (minicart.hasClass('active')) {
                            $(body).addClass('minicart-opened');
                        }

                        $(minicartBlock).on('dropdowndialogopen', function () {
                            scrollHtml = $(html).scrollTop() + $(body).scrollTop();
                            $(body).addClass('minicart-opened');

                            if ($(body).hasClass('mobile-menu-opened')) {
                                $('.mobile-menu-icon').trigger('click');
                            }
                        });

                        $(minicartBlock).on('dropdowndialogclose', function () {
                            $(body).removeClass('minicart-opened');
                            $(body).scrollTop(scrollHtml);
                            $(html).scrollTop(scrollHtml);
                        });
                    },
                    exit: function () {
                        $(minicartBlock).on('dropdowndialogopen', function () {
                            $(body).removeClass('minicart-opened');
                        });

                        $(body).removeClass('minicart-opened');
                    }
                });
            })
        </script>

<script type="text/javascript">
    require([
        'jquery',
        'mage/translate'
    ], function ($, $t) {
        /**
         * Header navigation:
         * - Handles copying of mobile menu content
         * - Manages active menu classes
         * - Adds 'plain-text' classes, if any of second level items lack URLs
         * - Takes care of 3rd level category windows in header menu (mobile)
         */

        // Clone the content of desktop navigation block to mobile + prepend back buttons first
        $('.mobile-navigation').html($('nav.navigation').clone());
        var secondLevelSubmenuMobile = $('.mobile-navigation .level1 > ul');
        secondLevelSubmenuMobile.prepend('<li class=\'level2\'><a class=\'back\'>' + $t('Go back') + '</a></li>');

        setActiveClasses();

        // Only After active classes have been set
        var firstLevelItems = $('.navigation .level0.level-top'),
            firstLevelItemLink = $('.navigation .level0.level-top > a'),
            firstLevelLinks = $('.navigation .level0 > a'),
            firstLevelSubmenu = $('.navigation .level0 > ul'),
            secondLevelLinksMobile = $('.mobile-navigation .level1 > a'),
            secondLevelSubmenu = $('.navigation .level1 > ul'),
            thirdLevelLinksMobile = $('.mobile-navigation .level2 > a'),
            thirdLevelSubmenuBack = $('.mobile-navigation .level2 > .back'),
            thirdLevelSubmenu = $('.navigation .level2 > ul'),
            fourthLevelLinksMobile = $('.mobile-navigation .level3 > a'),
            initialActiveSubmenu = $('.nav-container .navigation .level0.level-top.active > ul'),
            bp = 1024;

        bindClickEvents();
        bindHoverEvents();

        /**
         * Sets the active menu item classes
         */
        function setActiveClasses() {
            var currentUrl = decodeURI(location.protocol + '//' + location.host + location.pathname),
                activeElem = $('.navigation a[href="' + currentUrl + '"]');

            if (activeElem.length) {
                activeElem.parents('li.level0, li.level1, li.level2').addClass('active');
            } else {
                $('.navigation .level0.level-top.first').addClass('active');
            }

            $('.navigation .level1 > a[href=""]').addClass('plain-text');
        }

        /**
         * Binds (mostly - mobile) click events
         */
        function bindClickEvents() {
            firstLevelItems.click(function () {
                if (window.matchMedia('(max-width: ' + (bp - 1) + 'px)').matches) {
                    $(this).toggleClass('active');
                    firstLevelItems.not($(this)).removeClass('active');
                }
            });

            secondLevelLinksMobile.click(function (e) {
                e.preventDefault();
                $(this).next('.submenu').css('display', 'block');

                return false;
            });

            firstLevelLinks.click(function (e) {
                if (window.matchMedia('(max-width: ' + (bp - 1) + 'px)').matches) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    if ($(this).next('.submenu').children('.first.last').length !== 0 || $(this).next('.submenu').length === 0) {
                        $(this).parent().unbind('click');
                        location.href = $(this).attr('href');

                        return true;
                    }
                }
            });

            thirdLevelSubmenuBack.on("click", function (e) {
                $(this).parent().parent().css('display', 'none');
                e.preventDefault();

                return false;
            });

            secondLevelSubmenuMobile.click(function (e) {
                e.preventDefault();

                return false;
            });

            thirdLevelLinksMobile.click(function () {
                if (!$(this).hasClass('back')) {
                    if ($(this).next('.submenu').children('.first.last').length !== 0 || $(this).next('.submenu').length === 0) {
                        secondLevelSubmenuMobile.unbind('click');
                        location.href = $(this).attr('href');

                        return true;
                    }

                    $(this).toggleClass('is-open');
                    thirdLevelLinksMobile.not($(this)).removeClass('is-open');
                }
            });

            fourthLevelLinksMobile.click(function (e) {
                if (window.matchMedia('(max-width: ' + (bp - 1) + 'px)').matches) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    location.href = $(this).attr('href');

                    return true;
                }
            });
        }

        /**
         * Binds (Mostly desktop) hover events
         */
        function bindHoverEvents() {
            thirdLevelSubmenu.add(secondLevelSubmenu).hover(
                function () {
                    $(this).prev().addClass('has-open');
                },
                function () {
                    $(this).prev().removeClass('has-open');
                }
            );

            firstLevelSubmenu.not(initialActiveSubmenu).hover(
                function () {
                    if (window.matchMedia('(min-width: ' + bp + 'px)').matches) {
                        $(this).parent().addClass('active');
                    }
                },
                function () {
                    if (window.matchMedia('(min-width: ' + bp + 'px)').matches) {
                        $(this).parent().removeClass('active');
                    }
                }
            );

            firstLevelItems.hover(
                function (e) {
                    if (window.matchMedia('(min-width: ' + bp + 'px)').matches && !$(e.currentTarget).hasClass('active')) {
                        $(firstLevelItems).filter('.active').addClass('not-hovered');
                        $(e.currentTarget).addClass('hovered');
                    }
                },
                function (e) {
                    if (window.matchMedia('(min-width: ' + bp + 'px)').matches && !$(e.currentTarget).hasClass('active')) {
                        $(firstLevelItems).filter('.active').removeClass('not-hovered');
                        $(e.currentTarget).removeClass('hovered');
                    }
                }
            );

            firstLevelItemLink.hover(
                function (e) {
                    if (window.matchMedia('(min-width: ' + bp + 'px)').matches) {
                        $(e.target).addClass('hovered');
                    }
                },
                function (e) {
                    if (window.matchMedia('(min-width: ' + bp + 'px)').matches) {
                        $(e.target).removeClass('hovered');
                    }
                }
            );
        }
    });
</script>

        }">

<script type="text/javascript">
                require(['jquery', 'slick'], function ($) {
                    var customArrows = $('.slick-custom-controls-1549');
                    var desktopBP = 1023,
                        categoryElem = $('.homepage-top-categories-1549'),
                        slickVar = {
                            infinite: true,
                            dots: true,
                            appendArrows: customArrows,
                            appendDots: customArrows,
                            responsive: [
                                {
                                    breakpoint: 768,
                                    settings: {
                                        slidesToShow: 1,
                                        slidesToScroll: 1
                                    }
                                },
                                {
                                    breakpoint: desktopBP,
                                    settings: {
                                        slidesToShow: 2,
                                        slidesToScroll: 1
                                    }
                                },
                                {
                                    breakpoint: 99999,
                                    settings: "unslick"
                                }
                            ]
                        },
                        runSlick = function () {
                            if (categoryElem.hasClass('slick-initialized')) return;

                            categoryElem.slick(slickVar);
                        };

                    // initially
                    runSlick();

                    // slick 'unslick' re-init bugfix
                    $(window).on('resize orientationChange', function (event) {
                        if (event.target.innerWidth <= desktopBP) {
                            runSlick();
                        }
                    });
                });
            </script>

<script type="text/javascript">
            require(['jquery', 'slick'], function ($) {
                var customDots = $('.slick-banner-controls-3182'),
                    banner = $('#' + 'bluefoot-homepage-banner-3182'),
                    arrowPrev = banner.find('.prev-arrow-3182'),
                    arrowNext = banner.find('.next-arrow-3182');

                $('.homepage-banner-3182').slick({
                    prevArrow: arrowPrev,

<div class="bluefoot-static-block bluefoot-entity seo-text">
<p><img alt="Om Byggmax" data-lazy-load="https://cdn-webp.byggmax.no/media/wysiwyg/cms/team/team-1.png" height="348" width="608"/></p>
<h1><b>BYGGMAX – MER ENN BARE BILLIGE BYGGEVARER</b></h1>
<div>
<p><strong> Ideen vår er enkel: Vi tilbyr byggevarer av god kvalitet til en lav pris – uansett om du bare pusser opp hjemme eller er profesjonell. Vi tilbyr også byggeinstruksjoner, gjør-det-selv-filmer og inspirasjon. Uansett om du skal bygge veranda, legge gulv eller male om, finner du det du trenger hos Byggmax. </strong></p>
<p>På anleggene våre rundt om i landet finner du alt du trenger for å bygge et helt hus, fra grunnmur til tak. Men hvordan kan byggematerialet være så billig? Jo, fordi i byggevarehusene våre finner du ikke hundretusenvis av produkter som du ikke trenger (det finner du i stedet på nett), men kun et nøye utvalgt grunnsortiment.</p>
<p>Med den taktikken kan vi tilby deg de byggevarene du virkelig trenger, i store volumer og til en lav pris. Dessuten lar vi deg gjøre en del av jobben, som å plukke sammen det du selv vil ha og selv laste det i bilen i vår smidige drive-in. Smart, ikke sant?</p>
</div> </div>
</div>
